**26** pointers name a record that is not in the merged file, of which **0** are family-structure pointers (`CHIL`, `HUSB`, `WIFE`, `FAMC`, `FAMS`). Only the structural ones would mean a broken tree; the rest are incidental references to submitter or note records the exports did not include.
